HISTORICAL RESEARCH — hypothetical execution only

From order-book evidence to explicit simulation assumptions

pilot-5b363cb7828541a9

ResearchExecutionLimitsReproduce

How to read this report

Prediction quality, hypothetical trading performance and software correctness are separate assessments.

Read the research decision first, compare all declared execution scenarios, then inspect the assumptions and provenance. A positive classification comparison does not establish profitable trading.

Machine-readable aggregate evidence · Plain-text report

Research comparison

Historical observations; predictive conclusions are scope-limited and trading results use hypothetical execution assumptions.

Primary: book-feature logistic regression versus price-history-only logistic regression. Secondary: boosted trees versus book-feature logistic regression. The practical improvement threshold is declared before final evaluation.

Development planning only. Reserved final results have not been scored.

Declared practical improvement: 0.010000 macro F1. Precision target: interval half-width at most 0.015000.

On a narrow screen, swipe the table horizontally to see all columns. Keyboard: focus the table and use the left/right arrow keys.

Date-mean predictive scores
ModelDevelopment macro F1Final macro F1Final balanced accuracyFinal dates
majority0.204842Not reportedNot reportedNot reported
history_logistic0.296870Not reportedNot reportedNot reported
book_logistic0.347730Not reportedNot reportedNot reported
boosted0.372856Not reportedNot reportedNot reported

Scores range from 0 to 1; higher is better. Macro F1 gives each target class equal weight. Both development and final columns average per-date scores. History-only uses past midpoint changes and volatility; book models also use spread, depth and imbalance.

Model and research warnings (5)
  • One venue/instrument and fixed two-hour excerpts per selected date; results do not establish continuous-day or cross-market generalization.
  • Date-block uncertainty remains conditional on dependence/stationarity assumptions; shared expanding fits may induce longer dependence.
  • Macro-F1 uses all three fixed classes; balanced accuracy averages classes present on each true evaluation date.
  • Classification evidence is not evidence of executable profit. Economic results require the separate declared simulator and costs.
  • Local signals and fitted models are excluded from published aggregate artifacts.

Hypothetical execution

Historical observations; predictive conclusions are scope-limited and trading results use hypothetical execution assumptions.

Long/flat, unlevered spot; marketable IOC orders execute only on later observed books. Fees, adverse slippage and action delays are research assumptions. Queue position, market impact and exchange-realistic fills are not established.

No execution results in this development-only artifact.

Totals sum independent segment trials with capital reset. Unclosed inventory is marked at the segment’s observed best bid; it is not an executed liquidation. These totals are not a continuously investable equity curve.

Full aggregate execution metrics
{}

What the evidence does and does not support

  • This is hypothetical execution under declared assumptions, not observed fills or a profitability claim.
  • Eight selected 15-minute windows on each first-of-month date are not continuous four-year market coverage.
  • Date blocks and adjacent months are not proven independent or exchangeable; bootstrap conclusions are conditional on the stated dependence model.
  • Sessions start with equal cash independently. Segment-end inventory is marked at observed bid, not liquidated through unseen data.
  • Fees/slippage/delays are scenario assumptions, not historical account-specific rates or measured trading-system latency.
  • Top-five reconstructed snapshots cannot establish queue priority, hidden liquidity, market impact or exchange-realistic fills.
  • Predictive comparison, hypothetical execution outcome and software acceptance are reported separately.
  • Historical event data, signals, detailed ledgers and fitted model assets stay local under provider terms.
External method adaptation, not numerical reproduction
{}

Reproduce the evidence

Use the locked Python environment and obtain your own checksum-matching data under the provider terms. Detailed historical signals, orders, fills, ledgers and models remain local.

uv sync --frozen --extra dev
uv run --offline python -m market_microstructure.extension reproduce --reference reports/extension/reference/experiment.json --raw-dir data/extension/raw --output artifacts/extension/reproduction

The synthetic demo runs offline and requires no historical data. See the project README for the complete acquisition and experiment workflow.
